| `LiquidMorph` | The kit's smooth union (optics.md section 2); the Rayleigh–Plateau break-up of a liquid thread | With the kit's natural-log exponential smooth minimum (k a length), two outlines a gap g apart touch when k = g ÷ (2 ln 2). On a thread of radius R the fastest-growing ripple has kR = 0.697, a wavelength of 9.015 R; one wavelength's volume makes a drop of radius 1.891 R (inviscid ideal, no satellite drops). | With g = 65 px they touch at 46.89 px: apart at 20, one body at 60. R = 9 px gives a pitch of 81.1 px and drops of 17.0 px. One drop holds the volume of one pitch of thread. |
| `LorenzButterfly` | The Lorenz equations, σ = 10, β = 8 ÷ 3, ρ = 28 × rpm ÷ shift rpm; the model's clock is distance driven | x′ = σ(y − x), y′ = x(ρ − z) − y, z′ = xy − βz, by fourth-order Runge–Kutta, ρ clamped to [0, 28]. Resting points C± = (±√(β(ρ − 1)), ±√(β(ρ − 1)), ρ − 1), signs paired, for ρ > 1, stable until ρ_H = σ(σ + β + 3) ÷ (σ − β − 1) (subcritical Hopf). Transient chaos from ρ ≈ 13.926; the chaotic attractor exists from ρ ≈ 24.06, beside the stable C± up to ρ_H. On a gear change, or when ρ falls below 24 with a point more than 3 units from its eye, each point is set 0.5 units from its eye. | ρ_H = 24.7368 = 470 ÷ 19. At ρ = 28 the resting points sit at x = ±8.4853, z = 27. The two mirrored points stay mirror images bit for bit. At ρ = 10 a point settles on (4.899, 4.899, 9); at ρ = 28 it never settles. For ρ from 5 to 24 a point set 0.5 from its eye in any direction settles there (integrated: 60 of 60 at ρ = 5, 14, 20, 23 and 24). A point on the z axis at ρ > 1 is nudged off it. No square root of a negative number below ρ = 1. |
| `SuminagashiMarble` | Mathematical marbling: the ink-drop map and the tine-line map, both area-preserving | A drop of radius r at C sends P to C + (P − C)·√(1 + r² ÷ ‖P − C‖²), so distance ρ becomes √(ρ² + r²) (Jaffer). A stroke along a line moves a point by z·u^s along it, u = 2^(−1 ÷ c), s its distance from the line (Jaffer's tine line). | Ring k's edge is at √(100² + k · 90²): 134.5 px for k = 1 and 301.7 px for k = 10. Every ring holds π · 90² of area. Both maps keep the area of any patch (Jacobian 1, the drop's everywhere but at C). Neighbouring edges are never closer than 12 px (13.7 px between 9 and 10). Each map composed with its inverse returns the point. |
| `KaleidoscopeMirror` | Two first-surface mirrors at 180° ÷ n, the dihedral group D_n of order 2n; damped pendulums under a steady force | The wedge's 2n copies under the reflections (360° ÷ θ − 1 images plus the direct view); a copy that has met k mirrors keeps R^k of the light, R = 0.92. A chip of length L swings at √(g_eff ÷ L) ÷ 2π (undamped) with g_eff = √(g² + \|a\|²), a the horizontal force vector, limited to 1 g, and hangs at atan(a_lat ÷ g). | n = 6 gives 12 copies that match across every mirror. At rest L = 0.36 m and 1 m swing at 0.831 Hz and 0.498 Hz; at 1 g the 0.36 m chip swings at 0.988 Hz. At 1 g a chip hangs at 45.00°. The far copy at n = 10 keeps 0.92^10 = 0.434. |
| `GoldenAngle` | Vogel's model of the sunflower head | Seed n at radius c√n and angle n · α, α = 360° ÷ φ² = 137.5078° plus 0.25° per g of lateral force (positive to the right). At α = 360° · p ÷ q the seeds stand in q straight spokes, visible beyond radius c·q ÷ (2√π). | Seeds within radius ρ number (ρ ÷ c)², to within one. Each seed's nearest neighbours differ from it by Fibonacci numbers (13 to 55 over seeds 50 to 500). The convergents 8/21, 21/55 lie below α and 13/34, 34/89 above it: 34 spokes at +0.557 g, 89 at +0.081 g, 55 at −0.213 g, 21 at −1.460 g. 275 seed centres lie in the 1100 × 400 ellipse at c = 20, the outermost being seed 754. |
| `HopfFibration` | The Hopf fibration, by stereographic projection from (0, 0, 0, 1), a point on the fibre over the far pole | The fibre over the point at angle θ from the pole and longitude φ: z₁ = cos(θ ÷ 2) e^(i(ξ + φ)), z₂ = sin(θ ÷ 2) e^(iξ); the Hopf map (2z₁z̄₂, \|z₁\|² − \|z₂\|²) sends it to (sin θ cos φ, sin θ sin φ, cos θ). The fibres over the latitude circle θ weave a torus of ring radius 1 ÷ cos(θ ÷ 2) and tube radius tan(θ ÷ 2), in units of the projected near-pole fibre; each fibre has radius 1 ÷ cos(θ ÷ 2) and leans θ ÷ 2 from the torus plane. Tilted by τ, the tube runs from sin(θ ÷ 2) ÷ (cos(θ ÷ 2) + sin(τ ÷ 2)) to sin(θ ÷ 2) ÷ (cos(θ ÷ 2) − sin(τ ÷ 2)). | Every projected fibre but the far pole's is an exact circle. Two fibres link once: the Gauss linking integral gives ±1.0000 (sign by orientation). θ = 90° gives radii √2 and 1; θ = 40° gives 1.0642 and 0.3640; θ = 110° gives 1.7434 and 1.4281. At θ = 40°, τ = 25° the tube runs 0.296 to 0.473. A turn of 22.5° about the circle's axis maps fibre k onto fibre k + 1. |
| `HyperbolicTiles` | Regular tilings {p, q} of the hyperbolic plane, in Poincaré's and Klein's discs | (p − 2)(q − 2) > 4. The central tile's corners sit at tanh(R_h ÷ 2) in Poincaré's disc, with cosh R_h = cot(π ÷ p) cot(π ÷ q). A translation by t is z → (z + a) ÷ (1 + āz), \|a\| = tanh(t ÷ 2): it moves the centre to tanh(t ÷ 2) in Poincaré's disc and tanh t in Klein's (k = 2z ÷ (1 + \|z\|²)). Hyperbolic distance from the middle is 2 artanh\|z\|. The translation is a function of the present force, eased to at most t = 1.8; before it, the tiling symmetry nearest its inverse re-centres the patch. | {3,7} (first gear) and {7,3} (fifth): corner radius 0.300743; tile areas π ÷ 7 and π ÷ 3. {4,5}: 0.397975. {12,3}: 0.605. t = 1.2 gives 0.5370 and 0.8337. Every Poincaré edge meets the rim at 90° or is a diameter; every Klein edge is straight. With a patch out to 3.2 + R_h, re-centred, every point within hyperbolic distance 3.2 is covered for every t up to 1.8. Edges with both ends within 3.2: {3,7} 150, {4,5} 56, {5,4} 60, {6,4} 48, {7,3} 182, {8,3} 104, {12,3} 48. |

### Sci-Fi
| Dash | Model | The figure obeys | Its tests assert |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ChronoEventHorizon` | Light bending round a Schwarzschild black hole | Shadow edge at b_c = (3√3 ÷ 2) r_s. A deflection table α(b ÷ r_s) from the exact integral, built at load, serves every mass, so "mass is rpm" is a zoom of one lensing map. | b_c = 2.598076 r_s. α = 0.020300 rad at b = 100 r_s. |
| `SolarisWarpCore` | Plasma confinement: the magnetic mirror and the Z-pinch | Trapped fraction √(1 − 1 ÷ R_m). Bennett relation μ₀I² = 8πN k_B(T_e + T_i). Under adiabatic compression the column radius goes as I^(−3/2). The fallback is this model at a lower resolution, not curl noise. | Mirror ratio 4: loss-cone half-angle 30.00°, trapped fraction 0.8660. |
| `NeuralCyberSynapse` | A travelling nerve front (Nagumo); Rall's law for branches | v(x, t) = 1 ÷ (1 + exp((x − ct) ÷ √(2D))), c = √(D ÷ 2)(1 − 2a). Branch diameters: d_parent^(3/2) = Σ d_daughter^(3/2). | D = 1, a = 0.25 gives c = 0.35355. A symmetric branch is 0.6300 of its parent. |
| `EvaBioSync` | The single-track (bicycle) model | Expected yaw rate r = V δ ÷ (L + K_us V²). Yaw rate is not verified telemetry: until it is, the measured rate is a_lat ÷ V, which is exact only while the slip angle is steady, and the dash labels it `MODEL`. | Neutral steer with L = 2.7 m: gain 11.11 per second at 30 m/s. Hexagon ring k holds 6k cells. |
| `AeroPlasmaBlade` | Vortex lift of a slender delta wing | C_L = K_p sin α cos²α + K_v cos α sin²α. A streak wound by a vortex follows r = √(Γt ÷ (2πθ)). | Sweep 55° gives an aspect ratio of 2.801. The vortex's speed peaks at r = 1.1209 √(4νt). |
| `ArcLadderCascade` | The Marx generator and its RC charging ladder | V_out = n V₀. Charging is a discrete diffusion: C dV_k/dt = (V_(k−1) − 2V_k + V_(k+1)) ÷ R. | Twelve stages of 100 nF at 50 kV erect to 600 kV and hold 1,500 J. Stage k is half charged after about 1.1 k² RC. |

Brightness follows the vis-viva speed, v² ∝ 2 ÷ r − 1 ÷ a. θ is linear in rpm, so the craft is the exact reading. | r(0) = r₁ and r(π) = r₂. Speed at the low point ÷ speed at the high point = r₂ ÷ r₁. |

| `SynthwaveOutrun` (Retro) | Perspective of a ground-plane grid | A line at depth z sits f H_e ÷ z under the horizon. On a curve it shifts by κz² ÷ 2. | f = 900 px, eye height 1.2 m: lines at 5, 10, 20 and 40 m sit 216, 108, 54 and 27 px under the horizon. |

## Code re-cut
The code re-cut brought the code level with this page on 2026-11-28: `catalog.rs` holds the 72 rows with their category, shelf, target and build number, and the 24 added dashes have their themes (48 to 71), scaffold modules, registry rows, goldens, telemetry-matrix rows and empty asset-forge recipes. It was one Rust thread, started by the coordinator, and used no dash slot. What it did, for the record:

**The 24 added dashes.** Keys are final from the moment the re-cut is requested; afterwards only a display name or an OSD label may change. Theme values and catalog rows follow the build order of the added dashes.

**What the re-cut does**
1. **Themes.** Appends the 24 `ClusterTheme` variants with values 48 to 71 (`crates/fh6-core/src/theme.rs`). No existing value changes.
2. **Catalog as data.** `CATALOG` becomes `[CatalogEntry; 72]`. Each entry gains `category` (the nine categories, in the order of the [Categories](#categories) table), `shelf` (1 to 8, the order in the [Categories](#categories) table) and `target` (`Recreation`, `Inspired`, `Concept`), and takes its build number from this page. Tests: 8 dashes per category; shelf 1 to 8 once per category; builds 1 to 72 once each; the seven pattern-setters hold builds 1 to 7; every family's pattern-setter is built before its siblings.
3. **Names in one place.** `catalog.rs` is the only source of display names and the OSD reads it, so the second label table in `crates/fh6-osd/src/model.rs` goes. `AmgMbuxSuperscreen` shows as "AMG MBUX Superscreen". A test holds that display names are unique and none is the start of another. The aliases `amg` and `mbux` keep pointing at `AmgMbuxSuperscreen`.
4. **Modules.** Adds the 24 scaffold modules (`mod.rs`, `spec.md` with the brief from this page, `README.md`), their registry rows and their scaffold goldens.
5. **Build numbers leave the code's text.** The scaffold prints the catalog row and the category, and no module file names a build number, so a later change of order touches only this page and `catalog.rs`. The scaffold label `NEW CONCEPT` becomes `NEW DASH`.
6. **Telemetry matrix.** Adds 24 rows to `docs/TELEMETRY_USAGE_MATRIX.md` at the end, never reordering, with what `tests/dash_gates.rs` needs for them.
7. **Recipes.** Registers an empty recipe module for every dash that has none, in the three asset-forge lists. A dash thread then never edits those lists, and the sentence about them in `CLUSTER_PROMPT.md` changes in the same commit.
8. **Browse order.** `catalog.rs` exposes one browse order: category in TJX's order, then shelf. The `preview-export` gallery uses it, and the product page shows the category and the catalog row. Category browsing in the OSD is a follow-up for the OSD thread, not part of the re-cut.
9. **Psychedelic and Abstract colour budget.** `preview-export` measures the budget of a Psychedelic or Abstract dash outside its rank-4 figure, whose zone the dash names in its `Geom` table ([colour law](#psychedelic-and-abstract-pattern-first-colour-from-the-model), point 4).
10. **Scaffold baseline.** Benches one scaffold on the PC and writes `docs/perf/scaffold-baseline.md`, so the "at most 0.3 ms over a scaffold" rule keeps a baseline when no scaffold is left.
11. **Every "48".** Each count of dashes in the docs and skills (`AGENTS.md`, `ONBOARDING.md`, `docs/design/OSD.md`, the `fh6-hmi-design-rules` skill and whatever a search finds) becomes 72 in the same change, with the categories where a line-up is described.
12. **`spec.md` of unbuilt dashes.** Regenerates the header and brief of every scaffold module that has no thread, with its category, target and category note from this page.

**Limits**
- It touches no file of a dash that is done or has a thread. Where the two meet, the dash's side wins.
- Before its merge it rebases on `origin/main` and regenerates a golden only for a module that is still a scaffold and has no thread.
- After its merge it sends `MERGED code re-cut · <sha>` to the chain thread. The chain's next run finds the 24 modules and turns their rows from ⧖ to ☐.

## What the setup chat (07-00) put in place
- **Themes:** the 19 new concepts of Phase 3 are `ClusterTheme` variants 29–47, appended in the order of catalog rows 30–48 (`LumenEtch = 29` … `HarmonographDrive = 47`); the 29 existing values are unchanged. The 24 dashes of 2026-11-28 are appended as 48–71 by the [code re-cut](#code-re-cut).
- **Catalog as data:** `crates/fh6-clusters/src/catalog.rs` mirrors the build-order table (module name, family, kind, views, build order, ★). The registry, the golden tests and the tools read it; tests keep it in step with `ClusterTheme` and the registry. Since the code re-cut it holds all 72 rows in this page's build order, with category, shelf and target.
- **One directory per dash:** `crates/fh6-clusters/src/clusters/<snake_name>/` with `mod.rs` (the scaffold, replaced by the dash), `spec.md` (the brief, copied) and `README.md` (commands and ownership). The v1 tiny-skia originals moved to `src/legacy/`; Rust fix phase E removed them on 2026-11-27, so read one with `git show 21e8a06:crates/fh6-clusters/src/legacy/<snake_name>.rs`.
- **Registry:** `registry::REGISTRY` has a row per catalog row, each pointing at `clusters::<snake_name>::make`. Only the code re-cut edits it.
- **Shared components** (`crates/fh6-clusters/src/common/`, from [COMPONENTS.md](../../../docs/design/COMPONENTS.md)): HeroGear, TabularSpeed, ShiftLights pod optics and LimiterCue, GlassPlate (squircle, dimming pad, hood halo), the ribbon / light pipe, MicroLabel and LogoZone, the Tach variants (arc, bar, chevron), NeedleDial, Bezel, AdasRoad, GBall, TyreSlipQuad, SignedBar, AlarmColumn / DataTile, Seg7Digit, LiquidSurface, and the scaffold. Compose them; never fork them. A missing piece goes in your handover.
- **Per-dash commands:** `preview-export --cluster <Name>`, `cargo test -p fh6-clusters <snake_name>` (goldens `tests/golden/images/<Theme>[_hood]_<state>.png`), `FH6_WheelDash --demo --cluster <Name> --stats`.
- **Moves from Phase 3:** `F35HelmetHmd` is in the ADAS & HUD family, and `BoschDdu`, `ValkyrieLmp1` and `Gt7ModularTripod` form the Race Dash family (RESEARCH.md §10).
- **Telemetry honesty:** only fields `fh6_kit::Signals` carries or that `tests/golden/packets` verify (rpm, speed, gear, pedals, acceleration, velocity, power, boost) are safe today. Tyre slip, suspension, pitch / roll, fuel, lap times, torque and distance are **not** verified: derive from verified fields (as the pilot does: body slip for grip, integrated speed for distance) or verify the offset first (97% rule) and say so in your handover.
